Candidate update functions:
```
def natural_candidates(p,g):
    gscale = np.max(np.abs(g))
    if gscale<1e-15: return []
    gn = g/gscale
    cands=[]
    for lr in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]:
       logp = np.log(p) - lr*gn
       logp -= np.max(logp)
       new = np.exp(logp)
       new /= new.sum()
       cands.append(new)
    # also additive
    for lr in [0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1]:
       new = p - lr*gn # since gn O1; may negative
       new=np.maximum(new,0); s=sum; if s>0 new/=s; else skip
    return cands
```
But natural with gn normalized to max1, p_i can multiply by e^lr or e^-lr. lr=0.8 gives 2.2 change, okay. Evaluate each and choose best score. This is deterministic. It does not use momentum. Could add adaptive: maintain exponential averages of best accepted directions? Not necessary.

Implement adaptive lr:
```
lr = 0.1
for iter:
  compute q,M,score,g
  gn = g / max(abs(g))
  new = p * exp(-lr*gn); normalize
  newscore=score(new)
  if newscore < score - 1e-12:
      p=new; score=newscore; best...
      if score < best*(1-1e-6): update best
      # increase lr slightly up to max
      lr=min(lr*1.05, 1.0)
  else:
      lr=max(lr*0.5, 0.01)
  if stuck: random permute/restart
```

Let's design weights more concretely for large:
```
def weights_from_q(q, M, n):
    L = len(q)
    # top top10 uniform
    topK = min(L, max(20, int(0.002*n))) # ~11 for 5582? maybe 20
    idx = np.argpartition(q, -topK)[-topK:] if topK<L else np.arange(L)
    w_top = zeros; w_top[idx]=1/count; normalize
    # threshold: include q >= M - 0.3M
    thresh = 0.7*M
    mask = q >= thresh
    if mask.sum() < topK:
       # use topK
       w_main = w_top
    else:
       vals = np.maximum(q - thresh, 0)
       s=vals.sum(); if s>0 w_main=vals/s else w_top
    # alpha smooth
    alpha = 5.0 / max(M,1e-12)
    z = np.exp(alpha*(q-M))
    # prevent all mass on max? normalize; if sum=0 top
    w_soft = z/z.sum()
    w = 0.5*w_main + 0.3*w_soft +0.2*w_top
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```

Let's implement a function that returns weights based on q and mode. We can include parameter `wmode` integer.
```
def compute_weights(q, M, n, wmode=0):
    L=len(q)
    if M <= 0: uniform
    if wmode==0: # top rank + alpha
        topK = min(L, max(20, int(0.02*n)))
        idx = np.argpartition(q, -topK)[-topK:] if topK < L else np.arange(L)
        wtop = zeros; wtop[idx]=1.0; wtop/=count
        alpha = 10.0 / M
        z = np.exp(alpha*(q - M))  # min exp underflow? q-M>=0, values <=1, shift min -M maybe *alpha=-10 if q=0? Wait q-M min -M, alpha 10/M => -10. Good.
        s=z.sum(); if s>0: wsoft=z/s else wtop
        w=0.6*wtop+0.4*wsoft
    elif wmode==1: # broader alpha 3/M and threshold
       alpha = 3.0/M
       # avoid z for q<<0? q-M min -M => e^-3; OK.
       z=np.exp(alpha*(q-M)); normalize
       w=z
    elif wmode==2: # top threshold
       thresh=0.7*M
       vals=np.maximum(q-thresh,0); if sum>0 vals/=sum else top
```
But for large M=1/n, q-M for q=0 gives -M, alpha10/M -> e^-10=4.5e-5, fine. If M very small? M shouldn't. Use max M=1/n? if M<1e-12 uniform.

Update p:
```
def natural_update(p, gnorm, lr):
    logp = np.log(p+1e-300) - lr*gnorm
    logp -= np.max(logp)
    pnew = np.exp(logp)
    s=pnew.sum()
    if s<=0: return p*0.999? 
    return pnew/s
```

Let's define inner function:
```
def optimize_b_rule(p, Fp, p_max, inner_steps, alpha, lr, rng):
   b=p.copy()
   for _ in range(inner_steps):
      Fb=rfft(b,L); pb=irfft(Fp*Fb)[:2n-1]; M=max
      w=weights(pb,M,n)
      Fw=rfft(w,L); c=irfft(conj(Fp)*Fw)[:n]
      h=c  # derivative of pb wrt b
      hb=dot(b,h)
      g=h-hb
      g/=max(abs(g))
      update b natural with lr
      # periodically evaluate b*b? not
   return b
```
Since derivative of (p*b)_k wrt b_i = p_{k-i}; w weights. Good.

Pseudo top-level:
```
def optimize_direct(p0, deadline, holder, lr=0.1, wmode=0, tag=""):
    n=len(p0); p=normalize(p0); sc=score_fast(p); update_best(holder,p,sc, False)
    best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc
    local_best_holder? We can keep.
    fail=0; stagn=0; last_improve=time
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       q, M, sc = conv_stat(p)  # conv_stat returns q,M,score
       if sc < best_local_score: best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc; update_best
       w=make_weights(q,M,n,wmode)
       c=correlate(p,w)
       h=2*c; g=h - np.dot(p,h)
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gmax < 1e-14:
           p = add_noise(p, 0.1, rng); sc=fast_score(p); continue
       g/=gmax
       trials=[]
       for lr_t in (0.5*lr, lr, 2*lr, 4*lr):
          if lr_t>0: trials.append((lr_t, natural_update(p,g,lr_t)))
       best_p=p; best_sc=sc
       for lr_t, cand in trials:
          csc = fast_score(cand)
          if csc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              best_sc=csc; best_p=cand
       if best_p is not p:
          p=best_p; sc=best_sc; fail=0; last_improve=...
          # adjust lr based on chosen lr_t? We can store best_idx; if best_idx == last (larger) lr=min(lr*1.1,0.6) else lr=max(lr*0.8,0.02)
       else:
          fail+=1
          lr=max(lr*0.7,0.02)
          if fail>15:
              # perturbation restart from best_local or random
              scale=...; p=add_noise(best_local, scale)
              sc=fast_score(p)
              fail=0; lr=0.1
          elif fail%5==0:
              # alternating move
              b=inner_b(p, 20)
              for t in [0.3,0.6,0.9]: cand=normalize((1-t)*p+t*b); csc=fast_score(cand); if csc<sc: accept...
       if time>last_improve+60:
          # restart from best local with larger noise
          p=add_noise(best_local, 0.3); sc=fast_score(p); fail=0; lr=0.1
```
Need an RNG object passed to optimizer. `add_noise(p, scale, rng)` uses `p*(1+scale*randn)`? Since p sum1, scale relative to p? Let's define: `pert = scale * np.random.standard_normal(n)` where scale in probability units. If scale=0.05/n? For p_i~1/n, relative 0.05? Actually std = scale/n, scale 1 ->1% relative. Use `rel=0.1` gives scale=rel*mean(p)=rel/n. We can pass relative noise: `std = relative * p.mean()`. For relative 0.1, 10% Gaussian relative. For 0.5, large. Then `new = p + std*randn`; clip; normalize. If too many zeros, mix uniform.
```
def perturb(p, rel, rng):
    std = rel * (np.sum(p)/len(p))
    q=p + std*rng.standard_normal(len(p))
    q=np.maximum(q,0)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0: q=np.full_like(p,1/n)
    else q/=s
    # mix with uniform to avoid zeros
    q=(1-1e-8)*q + 1e-8/n
    return q
```

def inner_b(p, Fp, L, steps=20, lr=0.2, wmode=0):
   n=len(p); b=p.copy()

      if gmax<...: break

Implementation of `conv_stat`:
```
def conv_stat(p, L=None):
   n=len(p)
   if L is None: L=next_len(3*n-2) # for optimize
   Fp=rfft(p,L)
   q=irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:2*n-1]
   q[np.abs(q)<1e-18]=0.0
   M=float(np.max(q))
   if M<0: M=0.0
   score=2.0*n*M  # since sum=1
   return q,M,score,Fp
```
For grid/normal fast score, use `score_fast(p)` with Lq=next_len(2n-1). It can normalize p? We'll assume p sum≈1 but normalize maybe.
```
def score_fast(p):
   p=normalize(p)
   L=next_len(2*len(p)-1); Fp=rfft(p,L); q=irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:2n-1]; M=max(q); return 2*n*M
```

def next_fft_len(m):
   return 1 << (max(1,m)-1).bit_length()

Weights function using q, M, n, wmode:
We need user of q values maybe unnormalized sum? q sum=1. Use M. If M <=0 uniform. Use np.float64.
```
def make_weights(q, M, n, wmode=0):
   L=len(q)
   w=np.empty(L)
   if M <= 1e-300:
      fill 1/L return
   if wmode==0:
      topK = min(L, max(10, int(0.02*n)))
      if topK < L:
         idx=np.argpartition(q, -topK)[-topK:]
      else: idx=np.arange(L)
      wtop=np.zeros(L); wtop[idx]=1.0; wtop/=wtop.size
      alpha=8.0 / M
      z=np.exp(np.maximum(alpha*(q-M), -30.0))  # q-M can be large negative; but min -M? q>=0 so min -M, alpha => -8; safe.
      s=z.sum()
      if s>0: wsoft=z/s
      else: wsoft=wtop
      w=0.55*wtop + 0.45*wsoft
   elif wmode==1:
      alpha=3.0/M; z=np.exp(alpha*(q-M)); normalize
   elif wmode==2:
      thresh=0.75*M
      vals=np.maximum(q-thresh,0.0); s=vals.sum(); if s>0: w=vals/s else w=...
   else: ...
   s=w.sum(); if s<=0 uniform; else w/=s
   return w
```
For wmode0 alpha=8; topK uniform 55%, soft 45. Good. topK 0.02n for n=5582 =111. If q max unique, top 111 includes many below; this may be too broad? Uniform top 111 gives gradient average over top 111. Could help include secondary. Maybe reduce topK to max(10, int(0.005*n))=28. Let's choose 0.005. topK=28. Soft alpha=8 includes only within 12.5% of max. Good. Let's use 0.005.
But for n=2, topK=10? max(10) ->3 uniform top all. Good.

Could use several wmodes in different optimizer runs.

Natural update function:
```
def natural_update(p, g, lr):
    logp = np.log(p + 1e-300) - lr * g
    # subtract max for stability
    m = np.max(logp)
    q = np.exp(logp - m)
    s = q.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite: return p
    q /= s
    # mix tiny uniform
    q = (1.0 - 1e-12) * q + 1e-12 / len(p)
    return q
```
If lr up to 2 and gmax1, log change up to2, okay. For p very small 1e-12, log ~-27, subtract max, underflow possible but okay. Mix uniform.

`add_noise`:
```
def add_noise(p, rel, rng):
   n=len(p); std=rel*(n**(-1)) maybe p.mean()
   q=p + std*rng.standard_normal(n)
   q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
   s=q.sum()
   if s<1e-30: q=np.ones(n)/n
   else: q/=s
   q = (1-1e-10)*q + 1e-10/n
   return q
```
Use `p.mean()` more general.

Pseudo code for run_optimizer:
```
def run_optimizer(p0, deadline, holder, wmode=0, lr=0.05, tag=""):
    n=len(p0)
    p=normalize(p0)
    sc=score_fast(p)
    if sc < holder['score']: update_best(holder,p,sc)
    best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc
    fail=0; last_accept=time
    step=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       step+=1
       q,M,sc2,Fp=conv_stat(p)
       if sc2 < best_local_score - 1e-12:
           best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc2; update_best...
       if M <=0: break
       w=make_weights(q,M,n,wmode)
       c=correlate_from_fp(p,w,Fp)  # uses Fp length L
       h=2.0*c
       gp=np.dot(p,h)
       g=h-gp
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gmax < 1e-15:
           p=add_noise(p,0.1,rng); sc=score_fast(p); fail+=1; continue
       g/=gmax
       # candidate lrs
       best_p=p; best_sc=best_local? current sc? use sc2 (current true)
       best_lr=lr
       for mult in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
           lr_t = min(lr*mult, 0.8)
           cand=natural_update(p,g,lr_t)
           csc=score_fast(cand)
           if csc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=csc; best_p=cand; best_lr=lr_t
       if best_p is not p:
           p=best_p; current_sc=best_sc; fail=0; last_accept=time
           # adjust lr
           if best_lr > lr * 1.2: lr=min(lr*1.1,0.5)
           elif best_lr < lr*0.8: lr=max(lr*0.9,0.02)
           if best_sc < holder['score']: update_best
       else:
           fail+=1
           lr=max(lr*0.8,0.02)
           if fail == 10 or fail == 25:
               # try alternating inner b
               b=inner_b(p,Fp, steps=20, lr=0.2, wmode=wmode)
               for tt in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
                   cand=normalize((1-tt)*p + tt*b)
                   csc=score_fast(cand)
                   if csc < sc2:
                       p=cand; current_sc=csc; fail=0; last_accept=time; update_best; break
           if fail > 40:
               # restart from best local with perturbation
               p=add_noise(best_local, 0.2, rng)
               sc=score_fast(p); fail=0; lr=0.1
       if time.monotonic() - last_accept > 60:
           # long stagnation: random larger restart from best local or current best holder
           src = best_local if best_local_score <= holder['score'] else holder['p']
           p=add_noise(src, 0.5, rng)
           sc=score_fast(p); fail=0; lr=0.1
    return best_local
```

Now, improvement ideas beyond local: We can exploit the direct sum constraint and endpoint coefficients. The optimizer may get stuck at a point where the top active set has many lags but no gradient descent direction. Random perturbations help.

Maybe use deterministic random "coordinate pair" moves to find improvements. Implement `random_perturb` and maybe a `coordinate_accepted`:
- Choose a random active lag k (from top 5). For several pair i,j = k-i, transfer small mass from smaller to larger? Since q_k product xy, reducing product: For pair with x>y, move δ from y to x? Let's compute x'=x+δ, y'=y-δ. Product changes 2δ(y-x)-δ^2; if x>y, 2δ(y-x) negative, reduces. So move mass from smaller to larger (increase disparity). This can reduce active q. Also affects other lags. For each active top, choose random pair, δ = eps*sqrt(x*y) maybe relative 0.1. Update p, normalize, score; accept if improves. This is a local random search directly into promising directions. Could be useful when gradient fails. Implement in stagnation:
```
def random_gradient_perturb(p, q, topK, rel, rng):
   p=p.copy()
   for _ in range(10):
       k = int(rng.choice(np.where(q>=... top?)))
       imin=max(0,k-n+1); imax=min(n-1,k)
       i=rng.integers(imin,imax+1); j=k-i
       if i==j: skip
       x=p[i]; y=p[j]
       if x<y: i,j=j,i; x,y=y,x
       delta = rel * min(x,y) * rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)
       p[i]+=delta; p[j]-=delta
   p clip normalize
```
This transfers from smaller to larger on active diagonal, balancing? It makes distribution more unequal, reducing product. Could improve. But if q active at many diagonals, random may hurt. Accept only if score improves; we can try 20 such perturbations during stagnation. Cost each score FFT. We can limit.

Add if fail>20: generate 5 random perturbations and accept best if improves. Use time.

Maybe use a simple population: Maintain a list of top few sequences (size 5). Every so often, apply mutations and insert if better. This can avoid loss of diversity. But code complexity. We can implement a simple `improve_population`? Maybe not.

Let's think about numerical correctness of correlation using rfft: For real a,b length L padded, `irfft(np.conj(A) * B, n=L)` returns circular correlation `corr[k]=sum_j a_j b_{j+k mod L}`. With L sufficiently large (≥ len(a)+len(b)-1? For cross, len(a)=n, len(b)=2n-1, need L≥3n-2 to avoid wrap for k=0..n-1? The circular correlation at small k uses b_{j+k}; for j up to n-1, j+k up to j+n-1 ≤2n-2 if k≤n-1, within b length. Terms with wrap from j+k≥L could contaminate if L<3n-2. We set L next power two ≥3n-2, so clean. Good.

For q=p*p, need L≥2n-1; we use L≥3n-2 so okay.

For inner_b conv p*b (len n,n) need L≥2n-1; okay.

Memory for L with n=5582: next_pow2(16744)=32768. Good.

One more thought: The evaluate_sequence uses `np.convolve(sequence, sequence)`, which for list of Python floats returns full linear convolution. Our FFT uses zero-padding and no wrapping; good. However `np.convolve` for non-symmetric a*a output length 2n-1. Our q uses same. Score `2*n*M/(sum^2)`. With p sum1, score=2nM. If M tiny negative? no.

Potential issue: If p has sum=1 but due normalization, sum maybe 0.999999999, actual score slightly different. We can normalize exactly by `p /= p.sum()` and maybe after update. Good.

`make_from_params`:
```
def make_from_params(n, kind, a, b, c3, c4):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    eps=1e-7
    if kind==0:
       val=np.exp(-a*np.log(x+1e-6) + b*np.log(1-x+1e-6))
    elif kind==1:
       val=np.exp(a*np.log(x+1e-6)+b*np.log(1-x+1e-6)+c3*x+c4*x*x)
    else:
       val=np.ones(n)
    val=np.maximum(val,0.0)
    s=val.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return ones/n
    return val/s
```
For a,b log coefficients, ensure val not overflow; x range [0.5/n,1-0.5/n], log ~±5 for n=5000. Coeff ranges small, exp okay.

```
def run_optimizer(p0, deadline, holder, rng, wmode=0, lr_init=0.05, tag=""):
    n=len(p0); L=next_pow2_len(3*n-2)
    p=normalize_p(p0)
    sc=score_fast(p, L)
    if sc < holder['score']: holder['p']=p.copy(); holder['score']=sc
    best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc
    lr=lr_init; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        q,M,sc_current,Fp=conv_stat(p,L)
        if sc_current < best_local_score - 1e-12:
            best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc_current
            if sc_current < holder['score']: holder['p']=p.copy(); holder['score']=sc_current
        w=make_weights(q,M,n,wmode)
        c=correlate_p_w(p,w,Fp,L)
        h=2.0*c
        gp=float(np.dot(p,h))
        g=h-gp
        gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <= 1e-15:
            p=add_noise(p,0.1,rng); fail+=1
            continue
        g /= gmax
        best_p=p; best_sc=sc_current; best_lr=lr
        for mult in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            lr_t = lr * mult
            if lr_t < 0.005: lr_t=0.005
            if lr_t > 0.9: lr_t=0.9
            cand = natural_update(p, g, lr_t)
            csc = score_fast(cand, L)
            if csc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc=csc; best_p=cand; best_lr=lr_t
        if best_p is not p:
            p=best_p; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic()
            if best_lr > lr * 1.2: lr = min(0.6, lr * 1.1)
            elif best_lr < lr * 0.8: lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.9)
            if p[0]?? no
            if best_sc < holder['score']: holder['p']=p.copy(); holder['score']=best_sc
        else:
            fail += 1
            lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.85)
            if fail == 12:
               # alternate inner
               b = inner_b(p,Fp,L,steps=15,lr=0.2,wmode=wmode)
               for tt in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
                  cand = normalize_p((1.0-tt)*p + tt*b)
                  csc=score_fast(cand,L)
                  if csc < sc_current:
                      p=cand; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic(); lr=0.1
                      if csc < holder['score']: holder['p']=p.copy(); holder['score']=csc
                      break
            if fail == 25:
               # random active perturbations
               for _ in range(8):
                   cand = random_active_perturb(p,q,rng,rel=0.25)
                   csc=score_fast(cand,L)
                   if csc < sc_current:
                       p=cand; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic(); lr=0.05
                       if csc < holder['score']: update
                       break
            if fail > 50:
               src = best_local if best_local_score <= sc_current + 0.001 else holder['p']
               p=add_noise(src,0.3,rng)
               sc_current=score_fast(p,L); fail=0; lr=0.1
        if time.monotonic() - last_accept > 90:
            src = best_local
            p=add_noise(src,0.5,rng)
            sc_current=score_fast(p,L); fail=0; lr=0.1
    # at end, ensure best local in holder
    if best_local_score < holder['score']:
        holder['p']=best_local.copy(); holder['score']=best_local_score
```
Need `random_active_perturb` top-level:
```
def random_active_perturb(p, q, rng, rel=0.25, count=5):
   n=len(p); L=len(q)
   p2=p.copy()
   # choose top active indices: maybe use argpartition q top 20
   topK=min(L, max(20, int(0.002*n)))
   if topK < L:
      idx=np.argpartition(q, L-topK)[L-topK:]
   else: idx=np.arange(L)
   for _ in range(count):
       k=int(rng.integers(0,len(idx))) # klag = idx[random]
       klag=int(idx[k])
       lo=max(0, klag - n + 1); hi=min(n-1, klag)
       if hi<lo: continue
       i=int(rng.integers(lo, hi+1)); j=klag-i
       if i==j: continue
       x=p2[i]; y=p2[j]
       if x<y: i,j=j,i; x,y=y,x
       delta = rel * min(x,y) * float(rng.uniform(0.1,1.0))
       # move delta from j (smaller) to i (larger)
       p2[i] += delta; p2[j] -= delta
   p2=np.maximum(p2,0.0)
   s=p2.sum()
   if s>0: p2/=s
   # mix uniform
   p2=(1-1e-12)*p2+1e-12/n
   return p2
```
This transfers mass from smaller to larger on a random active diagonal. Good.

`inner_b` may use `make_weights` with pb M; if pb max maybe high. Good.

Potential issue: `score_fast` uses `normalize_p`, which mixes uniform? If we normalize after perturb, sum exactly 1. But `normalize_p` only divides, no mix. Candidate p from natural_update mixes. Good.

Define `normalize_p`:
```
def normalize_p(p):
    p=np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return p / len(p)  # but p zero, p/len zero -> bad
    p /= s
    return p
```
If zero, return uniform.
```
if s <= 1e-300:
   return np.full_like(p, 1.0/len(p))
```

`positive_mix` not needed separately.

`natural_update` adds 1e-10/n? We don't want alter sum/shape. Use `eps=1e-12`. `np.log(p+1e-300)`. After exp and normalize, mix `p2 = (1 - 1e-12) * p2 + 1e-12 / n` then sum might slightly >1; normalize again? Do:
```
p2 /= s
p2 = (1.0 - 1e-10) * p2 + 1e-10 / n
return p2
```
The sum is not exactly 1? (1-eps)*sum + eps =1. Good.

Let's write parameter_search carefully.
```
def parameter_search(n, deadline, rng):
    n_grid = min(n, 2048)
    pts=[]
    cnt=0
    # grid powers
    a_vals = np.arange(0.0, 0.81, 0.04)
    b_vals = np.arange(-0.5, 0.81, 0.05)
    for a in a_vals:
       for b in b_vals:
           p=make_from_params(n_grid,0,a,b,0,0)
           s=score_fast(p)
           pts.append((s,cnt,0,float(a),float(b),0.0,0.0))
           cnt+=1
           if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    # random logseq
    for _ in range(300):
       a=float(rng.uniform(-0.6,0.5))
       b=float(rng.uniform(-0.4,0.7))
       c3=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0))
       c4=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0))
       p=make_from_params(n_grid,1,a,b,c3,c4)
       s=score_fast(p)
       pts.append((s,cnt,1,a,b,c3,c4))
       cnt+=1
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    pts.sort()
    starts=[]
    for item in pts[:8]:
       _,cid,kind,a,b,c3,c4=item
       p_full=make_from_params(n,kind,a,b,c3,c4)
       sf=score_fast(p_full)
       starts.append((cid,kind,a,b,c3,c4,p_full,sf))
    return starts
```
No lambda. For `range(300)`, if time.

def smooth_p(p, half):
    n=len(p)
    if half <=0 or n <= 2*half:
        return normalize_p(p)
    kernel=np.ones(2*half+1)/(2*half+1)
    p2=np.convolve(p, kernel, mode='same')
    # linear convolution 'same' for even length? It returns same length, central.
    return normalize_p(p2)
```

Implement `score_fast`:
```
def score_fast(p, L=None):
    n=len(p)
    p=normalize_p(p)
    n_out=2*n-1
    if L is None:
        L=next_pow2_len(n_out)
    Fp=rfft(p,L)
    q=irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:n_out]
    q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
    M=float(np.max(q)) if n>0 else 0.0
    return 2.0*n*M
```
If p empty? n>0.

`conv_stat`:
```
def conv_stat(p, L=None):
    n=len(p); n_out=2*n-1
    if L is None: L=next_pow2_len(3*n-2)
    Fp=rfft(p,L)
    q=irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:n_out]
    q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
    M=float(np.max(q))
    return q,M,2.0*n*M,Fp
```
Do not normalize p here; assume.

`correlate_p_w`:
```
def correlate_p_w(p,w,Fp,L):
    n=len(p)
    Fw=rfft(w,L)
    c=irfft(np.conj(Fp)*Fw,L)[:n]
    return c
```
No need p. Keep for clarity.

`natural_update`: Need handle lr=0; if lr very small just return p? We can still.
```
def natural_update(p,g,lr):
   if lr <=0: return p.copy()
   logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - lr*g
   m=np.max(logp)
   q=np.exp(logp-m)
   s=q.sum()
   if s <=1e-300 or not np.all(np.isfinite(q)): return p.copy()
   q/=s
   q=(1.0-1e-12)*q + 1e-12/len(p)
   return q
```

`add_noise`: If rel=0 return copy. Use `np.isfinite`.
```
def add_noise(p, rel, rng):
   n=len(p); p=p.copy()
   if rel<=0: return p
   std=rel*(float(np.mean(p)) if n else 0.0)
   q=p + std*rng.standard_normal(n)
   q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
   s=q.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: q=np.full(n,1.0/n)
   else: q/=s
   q=(1.0-1e-12)*q + 1e-12/n
   return q
```

`random_active_perturb`: Use q maybe from current p. If q has L<len etc.
```
def random_active_perturb(p,q,rng,rel=0.25,count=6):
   n=len(p); p2=p.copy()
   topK=min(len(q), max(16, int(round(0.002*n))))
   idx=top_indices(q, topK)
   for _ in range(count):
       if idx.size==0: break
       k=int(idx[int(rng.integers(idx.size))])
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if hi<lo: continue
       i=int(rng.integers(lo,hi+1)); j=k-i
       if i==j or i<0 or j<0: continue
       x=p2[i]; y=p2[j]
       if x<y: i,j=j,i; x,y=y,x
       delta=rel*min(x,y)*float(rng.uniform(0.1,1.0))
       p2[i]+=delta; p2[j]-=delta
   p2=np.maximum(p2,0.0); s=p2.sum(); if s>0 p2/=s else uniform
   p2=(1-1e-12)*p2 +1e-12/n
   return p2
```

`make_from_params`: For kind 0, if a,b maybe negative, log. Use `x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n`. For n=1? x=0.5. Then logs.
```
def make_from_params(n, kind, a,b,c3,c4):
    if n <=0: return np.array([1.0])?
    x=(np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)+0.5)/float(n)
    eps=1e-7
    lx=np.log(x+eps); l1=np.log(1.0-x+eps)
    if kind==0:
       val=np.exp(-a*lx + b*l1)
    else:
       val=np.exp(a*lx + b*l1 + c3*x + c4*x*x)
    val=np.maximum(val,0.0)
    s=val.sum()
    if s<=1e-300 or not np.all(np.isfinite(val)):
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return val/s
```
For kind0 with a negative (i.e. x^|a|) fine. b can be negative.

`parameter_search`: Since `score_fast` normalizes, values no inf. For random logseq, if val overflow? Coeff ranges small, x log ~ -5, a<=0.6 ->3, exp safe. c3,c4 up to2*x^2 <=2. safe.
```
def parameter_search(n, deadline, rng):
    n_grid=min(n, 2048)
    pts=[]; cnt=0
    a_vals=np.arange(0.0, 0.81, 0.05)
    b_vals=np.arange(-0.5, 0.81, 0.05)
    for a in a_vals:
      for b in b_vals:
        p=make_from_params(n_grid,0,float(a),float(b),0,0)
        s=score_fast(p)
        pts.append((s,cnt,0,float(a),float(b),0.0,0.0)); cnt+=1
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
    # random
    n_rand = 250
    for _ in range(n_rand):
       a=...; b=...; c3=...; c4=...
       p=make_from_params(n_grid,1,a,b,c3,c4)
       s=score_fast(p)
       pts.append((s,cnt,1,a,b,c3,c4)); cnt+=1
    pts.sort()
    starts=[]
    for item in pts[:10]:
       score,uid,kind,a,b,c3,c4=item
       p_full=make_from_params(n,kind,a,b,c3,c4)
       sf=score_fast(p_full)
       starts.append((uid,kind,a,b,c3,c4,p_full,sf))
    return starts
```

`run_optimizer` code detail: At beginning, if `p0` length maybe 0? no.
```
def run_optimizer(p0, deadline, holder, rng, wmode=0, lr_init=0.05, tag="opt"):
    if len(p0)==0: return
    n=len(p0); L=next_pow2_len(3*n-2)
    p=normalize_p(p0)
    sc=score_fast(p,L)
    record_best_internal(holder,p,sc)
    best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc
    lr=lr_init; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        q,M,sc_current,Fp = conv_stat(p,L)
        if sc_current < best_local_score - 1e-12:
            best_local=p.copy(); best_local_score=sc_current
            record_best_internal(holder,p,sc_current)
        w=make_weights(q,M,n,wmode)
        c=correlate_p_w(p,w,Fp,L)
        h=2.0*c
        gp=float(np.dot(p,h))
        g=h-gp
        gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <= 1e-15:
            p=add_noise(p,0.1,rng); fail+=1; continue
        g = g/gmax
        best_p=p; best_sc=sc_current; best_lr=lr
        for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
            lr_t=lr*mult
            if lr_t < 0.005: lr_t=0.005
            if lr_t > 0.9: lr_t=0.9
            cand=natural_update(p,g,lr_t)
            csc=score_fast(cand,L)
            if csc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc=csc; best_p=cand; best_lr=lr_t
        if best_p is not p:
            p=best_p; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic()
            sc_current=best_sc
            if best_lr > lr * 1.2:
                lr = min(0.6, lr * 1.1)
            elif best_lr < lr * 0.8:
                lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.9)
            record_best_internal(holder,p,sc_current)
        else:
            fail += 1
            lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.85)
            if fail == 10:
                b = inner_b(p,Fp,L,steps=15,lr=0.2,wmode=wmode)
                for tt in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                    cand = normalize_p((1.0-tt)*p + tt*b)
                    csc = score_fast(cand,L)
                    if csc < sc_current - 1e-12:
                        p = cand; sc_current = csc; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic(); lr=0.1
                        record_best_internal(holder,p,sc_current)
                        break
            if fail == 25:
                for _ in range(6):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                    cand = random_active_perturb(p,q,rng,rel=0.25,count=8)
                    csc = score_fast(cand,L)
                    if csc < sc_current - 1e-12:
                        p=cand; sc_current=csc; fail=0; last_accept=time.monotonic(); lr=0.05
                        record_best_internal(holder,p,sc_current)
                        break
            if fail > 50:
                src=best_local
                p=add_noise(src,0.3,rng)
                sc_current=score_fast(p,L); fail=0; lr=0.1; last_accept=time.monotonic()
        if time.monotonic() - last_accept > 90:
            src=best_local
            p=add_noise(src,0.5,rng)
            sc_current=score_fast(p,L)
            fail=0; lr=0.1; last_accept=time.monotonic()
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"{tag}: iter={it} current={sc_current:.6f} local={best_local_score:.6f} best={holder['score']:.6f} lr={lr:.4f} fail={fail}")
    # final record best local
    if best_local_score < holder['score'] - 1e-12:
        record_best_internal(holder,best_local,best_local_score)
    print(f"{tag}: finished current={sc_current:.6f} local={best_local_score:.6f} best={holder['score']:.6f}")
```
Need `print` with f-string okay. If holder['score'] inf, f works.

In fail==10, `inner_b` might take time; no deadline inside inner. steps=15 okay. Could exceed deadline slightly; acceptable? We can pass deadline and break inside inner. Add `deadline` param to inner_b? To keep simple, steps=12. But if each inner step 4 FFTs, 15 quick. Fine.

`inner_b`: 
```
def inner_b(p,Fp,L,steps=15,lr=0.2,wmode=0):
    n=len(p); b=normalize_p(p)
    for _ in range(steps):
       Fb=rfft(b,L); pb=irfft(Fp*Fb,L)[:2*n-1]; pb=np.maximum(pb,0.0); M=float(np.max(pb))
       if M<=1e-300: break
       w=make_weights(pb,M,n,wmode)
       Fw=rfft(w,L); c=irfft(np.conj(Fp)*Fw,L)[:n]
       h=c; hb=float(np.dot(b,h)); g=h-hb; gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if gmax <=1e-15: break
       b=natural_update(b,g/gmax,lr)
    return b
```

`g_pair_i = ∑_k w_k (p_i - p_{k-i})`? For a single lag, pair direction per pair sum zero? For i and j=k-i, contributions: at i: p_i-p_j; at j: p_j-p_i. Sum over i zero. Derivative of q_k along d? q_k change ≈2∑ p_i p_{k-i} *? Earlier for one pair v=(x-y,y-x) derivative -2(x-y)^2. For weighted, direction d_i = ∑ w_k (p_i - p_{k-i}) (maybe). This makes each active diagonal more unequal, reducing q. Unlike gradient h=2p_shift, this may be better for nonconvex product. We can add candidate update from d:
```
d = zeros
for each weighted lag? We can compute via correlation? d_i = p_i * S_i - m_i? Where S_i=∑ w_k (for k such that k-i valid) -> count/lag, and m_i=∑ w_k p_{k-i}=c_i. So d_i = p_i * C_i - c_i, where C_i=∑_{k} w_k I(0≤k-i<n) = w convolved with box? C_i can be computed: for w length 2n-1, C_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} (sliding sum), same as c but with p_j=1. This is another cross-correlation of uniform vector and w. We can compute with Fw and FFT of ones? Or note C_i = sum of w over k from i to i+n-1. We can compute via cumulative sum of w: C_i = prefix[i+n]-prefix[i]. Since i=0..n-1, k range i..i+n-1 within 0..2n-2. Yes! Easy. Then c_i from correlation above. d_i = p_i * C_i - c_i. Because c_i = ∑_j p_j w_{i+j}, C_i∑_j 1*w_{i+j}. For each active lag k=i+j, pair difference weighted. This direction may reduce pairwise products. Sum d =∑p C - ∑c =? ∑_j p_j ∑_i? Not zero necessarily. For natural update, scale constant negligible. We can normalize d max. Add candidate `natural_update(p, d/|d|, lr)` alongside gradient. Does this direction have correct sign? For x>y, d_i=x-y positive, d_j=y-x negative. Natural update p_i*=e^{-lr d_i} decreases large, p_j increases small, which makes them more equal, increasing product! We want move mass from small to large, so update should be opposite: p_i *= e^{+lr (x-y)} increases large? But sum normalization? ```python
"""
Search for low peak self-convolution step function coefficients.

The searched objective is equivalent (by scale invariance) to

    2*n*max(conv(p,p))   with p >= 0, sum(p)=1.

The code uses a diverse set of structured/probabilistic starts, including
parent sequence, power-law / log-polynomial continuous approximations, and
asset-based local search with natural-gradient, pairwise-bias directions,
alternating inner directions, and random active-lag perturbations.  FFTs are
used for internal objective evaluations.
"""

import np_wrong as np  # placeholder removed below
```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft


def next_pow2_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(max(1, m))
    r = 1
    while r < m:
        r <<= 1
    return r


def normalize_p(p):
    """Nonnegative normalization to sum 1."""
    arr = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(arr.shape, 1.0 / float(arr.size))
    return arr / s


def top_indices(q, k):
    """Indices of k largest entries."""
    L = len(q)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    if k >= L:
        return np.arange(L)
    kth = L - k
    return np.argpartition(q, kth)[kth:]


def make_weights(q, M, n, wmode=0):
    """
    Weights over convolution coefficients.  Larger weights are assigned to
    near-maximum coefficients.  Several compatible variants are provided.
    """
    L = len(q)
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(1, L), dtype=np.float64)

    if wmode == 1:
        alpha = 3.0 / M
        z = np.exp(np.minimum(0.0, alpha * (q - M)))
        s = float(np.sum(z))
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            return z / s

    if wmode == 2:
        thresh = 0.75 * M
        vals = np.maximum(q - thresh, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(vals))
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            return vals / s

    if wmode == 3:
        topK = min(L, max(20, int(round(0.02 * max(1, n)))))
        idx = top_indices(q, topK)
        if idx.size == 0:
            return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(1, L), dtype=np.float64)
        order = idx[np.argsort(q[idx])[::-1]]
        ranks = np.arange(1, len(order) + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[order] = 1.0 / ranks
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            return w / s

    # default wmode 0: ranked top + soft active constraints
    topK = min(L, max(8, int(round(0.005 * max(1, n)))))
    idx = top_indices(q, topK)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(1, L), dtype=np.float64)
    order = idx[np.argsort(q[idx])[::-1]]
    ranks = np.arange(1, len(order) + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    wtop = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    wtop[order] = 1.0 / ranks
    s = float(np.sum(wtop))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        wtop = np.full(L, 1.0 / max(1, L), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        wtop /= s

    alpha = 8.0 / M
    z = np.exp(np.minimum(0.0, alpha * (q - M)))
    s = float(np.sum(z))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        wsoft = z / s
    else:
        wsoft = wtop

    w = 0.5 * wtop + 0.5 * wsoft
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(1, L), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def score_fast(p, L=None):
    """Fast objective value for sum-normalized p."""
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n_out = 2 * n - 1
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2_len(n_out)
    Fp = rfft(p, L)
    q = irfft(Fp * Fp, L)[:n_out]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * M


def conv_stat(p, L=None):
    """Return q, M, score, and FFT of p."""
    n = len(p)
    n_out = 2 * n - 1
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2_len(3 * n - 2)
    Fp = rfft(p, L)
    q = irfft(Fp * Fp, L)[:n_out]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
    return q, M, 2.0 * float(n) * M, Fp


def correlate_p_w(p, w, Fp, L):
    """
    c_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, using the same Fp used for p.
    """
    n = len(p)
    Fw = rfft(w, L)
    c = irfft(np.conj(Fp) * Fw, L)[:n]
    return c


def natural_update(p, g, lr):
    """Multiplicative natural-gradient style update in the simplex."""
    if len(p) == 0:
        return p.copy()
    if lr <= 0.0:
        q = p.copy()
    else:
        logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)) - lr * g
        m = float(np.max(logp))
        q = np.exp(logp - m)
        s = float(np.sum(q))
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
            return p.copy()
        q = q / s
    # Keep strictly positive to avoid log singularities.
    instance = (1.0 - 1e-12) * q + (1e-12 / float(len(q)))
    return instance


def add_noise(p, rel, rng):
    """Relative Gaussian perturb on the simplex."""
    p = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    if rel <= 0.0:
        return normalize_p(p)
    mean = float(np.mean(p))
    std = rel * max(mean, 1e-300)
    q = p + std * rng.standard_normal(n)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        q = np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    else:
        q = q / s
    q = (1.0 - 1e-12) * q + (1e-12 / n)
    return q


def inner_b(p, Fp, L, steps=15, lr=0.2, wmode=0):
    """
    Inner multiplicative search for b minimizing weighted max of p*b.
    This is a lightweight replacement for the active-set LP direction.
    """
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return p.copy()
    b = normalize_p(p)
    n_out = 2 * n - 1
    for _ in range(steps):
        Fb = rfft(b, L)
        pb = irfft(Fp * Fb, L)[:n_out]
        pb = np.maximum(pb, 0.0)
        M = float(np.max(pb)) if pb.size else 0.0
        if M <= 1e-300:
            break
        w = make_weights(pb, M, n, wmode)
        Fw = rfft(w, L)
        c = irfft(np.conj(Fp) * Fw, L)[:n]
        h = c
        gp = float(np.dot(b, h))
        g = h - gp
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <= 1e-15:
            break
        b = natural_update(b, g / gmax, lr)
    return b


def random_active_perturb(p, q, rng, rel=0.25, count=8):
    """
    Random coordinate-transfer moves on active diagonals.  For an active
    lag k, moving mass from the smaller coordinate to the larger one tends
    to lower the pairwise product contribution.
    """
    n = len(p)
    p2 = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if n == 0:
        return p2
    topK = min(len(q), max(16, int(round(0.002 * n))))
    idx = top_indices(q, topK)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return normalize_p(p2)
    for _ in range(count):
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        k = int(idx[int(rng.integers(idx.size))])
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        i = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
        j = k - i
        if i == j or i < 0 or j < 0 or i >= n or j >= n:
            continue
        x = float(p2[i])
        y = float(p2[j])
        if x < y:
            i, j = j, i
            x, y = y, x
        delta = rel * min(x, y) * float(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0))
        p2[i] += delta
        p2[j] -= delta
    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p2))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p2 = np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    else:
        p2 = p2 / s
    p2 = (1.0 - 1e-12) * p2 + (1e-12 / n)
    return p2


def smooth_p(p, half):
    """Small linear smoothing; sometimes reduces peaks."""
    p = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0 or half <= 0:
        return normalize_p(p)
    kernel = np.ones(2 * half + 1, dtype=np.float64) / float(2 * half + 1)
    p2 = np.convolve(p, kernel, mode="same")
    return normalize_p(p2)


def record_best_internal(holder, p, score):
    """Track best internal sequence and keep a short trail for final checks."""
    if score < holder["score"] - 1e-15:
        p = normalize_p(p)
        holder["p"] = p.copy()
        holder["score"] = float(score)
        holder["trail"].append(p.copy())
        if len(holder["trail"]) > 10:
            holder["trail"] = holder["trail"][-10:]


def run_optimizer(p0, deadline, holder, rng, wmode=0, lr_init=0.05, tag="opt"):
    """
    Local search:
      - gradient direction from weighted active coefficients
      - pairwise-bias direction on active diagonals
      - occasional inner b direction
      - random active-lag coordinate moves
    """
    if len(p0) == 0:
        return
    n = len(p0)
    L = next_pow2_len(3 * n - 2)
    p = normalize_p(p0)
    sc = score_fast(p, L)
    record_best_internal(holder, p, sc)

    best_local = p.copy()
    best_local_score = sc
    lr = float(lr_init)
    fail = 0
    last_accept = time.monotonic()
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        q, M, sc_current, Fp = conv_stat(p, L)
        if sc_current < best_local_score - 1e-12:
            best_local = p.copy()
            best_local_score = sc_current
            record_best_internal(holder, p, sc_current)

        w = make_weights(q, M, n, wmode)
        c = correlate_p_w(p, w, Fp, L)

        directions = []

        # Standard gradient-like direction for p*p.
        h = 2.0 * c
        gp_avg = float(np.dot(p, h))
        g1 = h - gp_avg
        gmax1 = float(np.max(np.abs(g1))) if g1.size else 0.0
        if gmax1 > 1e-15:
            directions.append(g1 / gmax1)

        # Pairwise-bias direction: for each active diagonal, make the two
        # paired entries more unequal, which lowers their pairwise product.
        prefix = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        prefix[0] = 0.0
        np.cumsum(w, out=prefix[1:])
        C = prefix[n:2 * n] - prefix[:n]
        gp = c - p * C
        gp_const = float(np.dot(p, gp))
        gp = gp - gp_const
        gmax2 = float(np.max(np.abs(gp))) if gp.size else 0.0
        if gmax2 > 1e-15:
            directions.append(gp / gmax2)

        if len(directions) == 0:
            p = add_noise(p, 0.1, rng)
            fail += 1
            continue

        best_p = p
        best_sc = sc_current
        best_lr = lr
        for d in directions:
            for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                lr_t = lr * mult
                if lr_t < 0.005:
                    lr_t = 0.005
                if lr_t > 0.9:
                    lr_t = 0.9
                cand = natural_update(p, d, lr_t)
                csc = score_fast(cand, L)
                if csc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = csc
                    best_p = cand
                    best_lr = lr_t

        if best_p is not p:
            p = best_p
            sc_current = best_sc
            fail = 0
            last_accept = time.monotonic()
            if best_lr > lr * 1.2:
                lr = min(0.6, lr * 1.1)
            elif best_lr < lr * 0.8:
                lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.9)
            record_best_internal(holder, p, sc_current)
        else:
            fail += 1
            lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.85)

            if fail == 10:
                b = inner_b(p, Fp, L, steps=12, lr=0.18, wmode=wmode)
                for tt in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                        break
                    cand = normalize_p((1.0 - tt) * p + tt * b)
                    csc = score_fast(cand, L)
                    if csc < sc_current - 1e-12:
                        p = cand
                        sc_current = csc
                        fail = 0
                        last_accept = time.monotonic()
                        lr = 0.1
                        record_best_internal(holder, p, sc_current)
                        break

            if fail == 25:
                for _ in range(6):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                        break
                    cand = random_active_perturb(p, q, rng, rel=0.25, count=8)
                    csc = score_fast(cand, L)
                    if csc < sc_current - 1e-12:
                        p = cand
                        sc_current = csc
                        fail = 0
                        last_accept = time.monotonic()
                        lr = 0.05
                        record_best_internal(holder, p, sc_current)
                        break

            if fail > 50:
                p = add_noise(best_local, 0.3, rng)
                sc_current = score_fast(p, L)
                fail = 0
                lr = 0.1
                last_accept = time.monotonic()

        if time.monotonic() - last_accept > 90:
            p = add_noise(best_local, 0.5, rng)
            sc_current = score_fast(p, L)
            fail = 0
            lr = 0.1
            last_accept = time.monotonic()

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(
                f"{tag}: iter={it} current={sc_current:.6f} "
                f"local={best_local_score:.6f} best={holder['score']:.6f} "
                f"lr={lr:.4f} fail={fail}"
            )

    if best_local_score < holder["score"] - 1e-12:
        record_best_internal(holder, best_local, best_local_score)
    print(
        f"{tag}: finished current={sc_current:.6f} "
        f"local={best_local_score:.6f} best={holder['score']:.6f}"
    )


def make_from_params(n, kind, a, b, c3, c4):
    """
    Structured probability sequences.

    kind=0: p_i proportional to x_i**(-a) * (1-x_i)**b
    kind=1: p_i proportional to exp(a*log(x) + b*log(1-x) + c3*x + c4*x*x)
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = 1e-7
    lx = np.log(x + eps)
    l1 = np.log(1.0 - x + eps)
    if kind == 0:
        val = np.exp(-a * lx + b * l1)
    else:
        val = np.exp(a * lx + b * l1 + c3 * x + c4 * x * x)
    val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(val))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.all(np.isfinite(val)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n))
    return val / s


def parameter_search(n, deadline, rng):
    """
    Search a small family of analytic shapes.  Returns a list of
    (uid, kind, a, b, c3, c4, p_full, full_score) entries.
    """
    n_grid = min(n, 2048)
    pts = []
    cnt = 0

    # Power-law family.
    a_vals = np.arange(0.0, 0.81, 0.05)
    b_vals = np.arange(-0.5, 0.81, 0.05)
    for a in a_vals:
        for b in b_vals:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            pf = make_from_params(n_grid, 0, float(a), float(b), 0.0, 0.0)
            s = score_fast(pf)
            pts.append((s, cnt, 0, float(a), float(b), 0.0, 0.0))
            cnt += 1
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    # Random log-polynomial family.
    for _ in range(250):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        a = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.5))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-0.4, 0.7))
        c3 = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        c4 = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        pf = make_from_params(n_grid, 1, a, b, c3, c4)
        s = score_fast(pf)
        pts.append((s, cnt, 1, a, b, c3, c4))
        cnt += 1

    pts.sort()
    starts = []
    for item in pts[:12]:
        _, uid, kind, a, b, c3, c4 = item
        p_full = make_from_params(n, kind, float(a), float(b), float(c3), float(c4))
        sf = score_fast(p_full)
        starts.append((uid, kind, float(a), float(b), float(c3), float(c4), p_full, sf))
    return starts


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    total_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    holder = {"p": None, "score": float("inf"), "trail": []}

    # Obtain parent length / sequence if available.
    n = 5582
    parent = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0:
            arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
            if arr.size > 0:
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                s = float(np.sum(arr))
                if s > 1e-300:
                    n = int(arr.size)
                    parent = arr / s
                else:
                    parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n = 5582
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    parent = normalize_p(parent)
    parent_internal = score_fast(parent)
    record_best_internal(holder, parent, parent_internal)
    print(f"n={n}, parent internal score={parent_internal:.6f}")

    # Analytic/structured starting points.
    search_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + 30.0, total_deadline - 60.0)
    param_starts = []
    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline:
        param_starts = parameter_search(n, search_deadline, rng)

    init_list = [(parent_internal, 0, parent.copy())]
    for idx, st in enumerate(param_starts):
        p_st = st[6]
        s_st = st[7]
        init_list.append((s_st, idx + 1, p_st.copy()))

    init_list.sort()

    # Phased local optimization.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = total_deadline - now
    if rem > 90.0 and init_list:
        phase1_end = now + 0.45 * rem
        run_optimizer(init_list[0][2], phase1_end, holder, rng, wmode=0,
                      lr_init=0.05, tag="main0")

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = total_deadline - now
    if rem > 60.0 and holder["p"] is not None:
        phase2_end = now + 0.42 * rem
        run_optimizer(holder["p"].copy(), phase2_end, holder, rng, wmode=1,
                      lr_init=0.07, tag="refine1")

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = total_deadline - now
    if rem > 40.0 and holder["p"] is not None:
        phase3_end = total_deadline - max(25.0, 0.02 * rem)
        run_optimizer(holder["p"].copy(), phase3_end, holder, rng, wmode=3,
                      lr_init=0.10, tag="refine3")

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = total_deadline - now
    if rem > 20.0 and holder["p"] is not None:
        phase4_end = total_deadline - 20.0
        run_optimizer(holder["p"].copy(), phase4_end, holder, rng, wmode=0,
                      lr_init=0.03, tag="final")

    # Build a final candidate set and verify with the official evaluator.
    cand_list = []
    if holder["p"] is not None:
        cand_list.append(holder["p"])
    for p_tr in holder.get("trail", []):
        cand_list.append(p_tr)
    cand_list.append(parent)
    for st in param_starts[:3]:
        cand_list.append(st[6])

    best_p = holder["p"] if holder["p"] is not None else parent
    for half in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        cand_list.append(smooth_p(best_p, half))

    filtered = []
    uid = 0
    for p in cand_list:
        if p is None or len(p) == 0:
            continue
        pn = normalize_p(p)
        if len(pn) != n:
            continue
        s = score_fast(pn)
        filtered.append((s, uid, pn.copy()))
        uid += 1

    filtered.sort()

    best_out = None
    best_out_score = float("inf")
    seen = 0
    for _, _, p in filtered[:10]:
        seq = [float(x) for x in p]
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_out_score:
            best_out_score = float(sc)
            best_out = seq
            seen += 1
        if best_out_score < 1.5027:
            # Still allow a few more to see if we can beat it, but do not spend all time.
            if seen >= 6:
                break

    if best_out is None:
        base = holder["p"] if holder["p"] is not None else parent
        best_out = [float(max(0.0, x)) for x in base]

    print(f"Final returned internal score={score_fast(np.asarray(best_out, dtype=np.float64)):.6f}, "
          f"best verified={best_out_score:.6f}")
    return best_out
```
